- **Recommendation**: Detector returns raw probabilities/score only; apply exactly one threshold in `VisionPipeline` (config-driven); GUI reads `pipeline.config().pad_threshold` for display; add `pad_score: f32` to `AuthContext` and let the policy apply `pad_threshold` (so policy tests cover it). Test: `test_vision_pipeline_default_thresholds_calibrated` extended to GUI/CLI construction sites.
- **Verifier notes**: Verified: crates/gui/src/main.rs:92 (not :288) `OrtPadDetector::new(pad_session, 0.80)` with `VisionPipelineConfig::default()` (main.rs:95-100) whose pad_threshold is 0.85 (vision/src/pipeline.rs:44); crates/gui/src/worker.rs:78-84 gates enrollment on `pipeline.config().pad_threshold` (0.85); crates/gui/src/app.rs:307 and :462 hardcode `p.score >= 0.80` for the on-screen label; crates/enrollment-cli/src/service.rs:773 uses 0.80 with the default pipeline config (0.85); `grep -n pad crates/policy/src/decision.rs` -> only `pad_passed` (decision.rs:14,28,35,118); `ThresholdConfig::pad_threshold` is never read by `evaluate`. Precision: the detector's 0.80 is not strictly dead but is dominated by the pipeline's 0.85 (effective threshold = max of the two); a frame with score in [0.80, 0.85) shows LIVE in the GUI label but is rejected by the pipeline, exactly the calibration confusion described.

#### PAD-11 — soos-enroll verify turns a PAD spoof into a generic error instead of a Deny report with PAD status

GitHub issue: #216

**MINOR** · CONFIRMED · bug · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/enrollment-cli/src/service.rs:521-522`, `crates/enrollment-cli/src/main.rs:58-82`, `Docs/ENROLLMENT_CLI.md:50`
- **Evidence**: service.rs:522 `Err(e) => Err(e.into()),` — `VisionError::PadFailed` is not matched, unlike NoFaceDetected/MultipleFaces/FaceBelowConfidence which build a `DiagnosticVerificationReport` with `pad_result` strings. main.rs:81 exits 1 on non-Allow, so adversarial_test.sh still counts it as rejected, but the report (score, latency, `pad_result: "SPOOF(...)"`) is never printed. Docs/ENROLLMENT_CLI.md:50 promises 'Presentation Attack Detection (PAD) status'.
- **Impact**: The only CLI diagnostic for PAD cannot show the PAD score for spoof presentations, making calibration on real hardware impossible from the CLI.
- **Recommendation**: Add an explicit `Err(VisionError::PadFailed { score, threshold })` arm returning `Verdict::Deny` with `pad_result: format!("SPOOF(score={score:.3}, threshold={threshold:.2})")`; test `test_verify_spoof_reports_deny_with_pad_score` with `MockPadDetector::new_spoof`.
- **Verifier notes**: `grep -n PadFailed crates/enrollment-cli/src/*.rs` -> no hits. crates/enrollment-cli/src/service.rs:494-528 has explicit report-building arms for MultipleFacesDetected and FaceBelowConfidence (and NoFaceDetected above), then :529 `Err(e) => Err(e.into())` (cited as 521-522; actual 529) swallows `VisionError::PadFailed { score, threshold }`. crates/enrollment-cli/src/main.rs:81-83 exits 1 when verdict != Allow, so a spoof surfaces as a generic error with no PAD score. Docs/ENROLLMENT_CLI.md:50 promises 'Presentation Attack Detection (PAD) status'. Confirmed.

#### PAD-12 — Guided enrollment gates each sample on single-frame liveness; a spoof frame is not fatal to the session

GitHub issue: #217

**MINOR** · CONFIRMED · security · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/gui/src/worker.rs:78-84`, `crates/enrollment-cli/src/guided_enrollment.rs:108-124`
- **Evidence**: worker.rs:78-84 computes `is_live` from the current frame only; guided_enrollment.rs:119-121 `if !is_live { return EnrollmentStepFeedback::SpoofDetected; }` simply skips the frame; the next frame that passes is accepted as a template sample.
- **Impact**: Enrollment templates can accumulate samples from a presentation that only intermittently passes PAD (root-only, so exploitation requires privilege, but it undermines template quality and the threat model's 'live enrollment' assumption).
- **Recommendation**: Track liveness over the session: require >= k consecutive live frames before accepting a sample and abort (or reset the step) when a spoof-classified frame occurs; test in guided_enrollment tests.
- **Verifier notes**: crates/gui/src/worker.rs:78-84 derives `is_live` from the current frame's `analysis.pad_result` only; crates/enrollment-cli/src/guided_enrollment.rs:108-121 `process_sample` returns `EnrollmentStepFeedback::SpoofDetected` when `!is_live` and otherwise continues, with no session-level spoof memory; the next passing frame is accepted. Root-only surface, MINOR is appropriate.

#### PAD-13 — No face-size or image-quality gate before PAD

GitHub issue: #218

**MINOR** · CONFIRMED · design · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/vision/src/pipeline.rs:289-311`, `crates/vision/src/pipeline.rs:313-334`
- **Evidence**: `grep -rn -i 'min_face_size|face_size|too small' crates/vision/src crates/inference-ort/src/detector.rs crates/daemon/src` -> no hits. process_frame only checks `detection.score < min_face_confidence`; a 30 px face is expanded 2.7x and upsampled to 80x80.
- **Impact**: Tiny or blurred faces (typical of a phone held at arm's length, or a print seen from far) produce unreliable PAD scores in both directions; also the 'shift inward' logic in crop.rs makes near-border faces get asymmetric context.
- **Recommendation**: Add `min_face_width_px` (or fraction of frame width, e.g. 0.12) and a cheap sharpness metric (variance of Laplacian on the 80x80 crop) to `VisionPipelineConfig`; return `VisionError::FaceTooSmall`/`FaceBlurred` mapped to Deny + a new ReasonClass or `FaceBelowConfidence`. Tests with fixtures at 30 px and 120 px.
- **Verifier notes**: `grep -rn -i 'min_face_size|face_size|too small|min_face_width|laplacian|blur' crates/vision/src crates/inference-ort/src crates/daemon/src` -> no hits. crates/vision/src/pipeline.rs:303-308 only gates on `detection.score < min_face_confidence`; the 2.7x expansion at :313-318 and `crop_and_resize` to 80x80 at :320-327 upsample any size. detector.rs:200 `min_sizes` are SCRFD anchor sizes, not a quality gate. Confirmed.

#### PAD-14 — Spoof attempts are not captured as evidence, only password failures are

GitHub issue: #261

**SUGGESTION** · CONFIRMED · improvement · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:685-692`, `crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:278-299`
- **Evidence**: dispatcher.rs:685-692 logs `debug!(score, threshold, "Presentation attack detected (PAD failed)")` and nothing else; evidence snapshots are stored only in `handle_event` for `EventKind::PasswordFailed` (278-299).
- **Impact**: The single strongest intrusion signal (a presentation attack) leaves no evidence; the evidence store's stated purpose ('intrusion snapshots') is not served.
- **Recommendation**: When the aggregated verdict is Deny/PadFailed and evidence is enabled, store one snapshot per request (respecting `daily_cap_per_uid`) with reason 'PadFailed'; log at info without scores of the template. Integration test mirroring test_12_4.
- **Verifier notes**: crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:271-299: evidence snapshots are stored only inside `handle_event` when `event.kind == EventKind::PasswordFailed`; `grep -rn PadFailed crates/daemon/src crates/evidence-store/src` -> only dispatcher.rs:685 (the `debug!` arm, no store call). Confirmed as a SUGGESTION; note any implementation must respect the opt-in `evidence.enabled` gate and `daily_cap_per_uid` (config.rs:270-285).

#### PAD-16 — Final reason is the last frame's reason, so a detected spoof followed by no-face frames is reported as NoFace

GitHub issue: #262

**SUGGESTION** · CONFIRMED · improvement · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:746-748`, `crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:789-798`, `crates/pam/src/lib.rs:118-119`
- **Evidence**: dispatcher.rs:746-748 `last_ctx = ctx; last_verdict = verdict; last_reason = reason_class;` on every evaluated frame; final response uses `last_reason` (794-798). PAM shows '[soos] Biometric spoof detected.' only for `ReasonClass::PadFailed` (pam/src/lib.rs:118).
- **Impact**: Audit logs and lock-screen feedback lose the spoof signal when the attacker withdraws the device before the budget ends.
- **Recommendation**: Keep a `spoof_seen: bool` (and max/min PAD scores) across the loop and prefer PadFailed as the final reason when set; unit test on the dispatcher with MockPadDetector spoof then detector empty.
- **Verifier notes**: crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:746-748 overwrite `last_ctx/last_verdict/last_reason` on every evaluated frame; :789-798 return `last_reason` when the loop ends without Allow; crates/pam/src/lib.rs:118-121 prints '[soos] Biometric spoof detected.' only for `ReasonClass::PadFailed`. A spoof frame followed by no-face frames is reported as NoFace. Confirmed; low impact (audit/UX only), SUGGESTION stands.

#### CAM-01 — PreviewFrame streams raw camera frames over IPC to any soos-group member with no peer, session or opt-in check

GitHub issue: #143

**CRITICAL** · CONFIRMED · security · effort M

- **Locations**: `crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:346-413`, `crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:415-461`, `crates/daemon/src/config.rs:28-32`, `AI/ARCHITECTURE.md:303`, `crates/gui/src/ipc_camera.rs:117-125`
- **Evidence**: dispatcher.rs Step 5c (`if req.kind == RequestKind::PreviewFrame { ... return Ok(...) }`, lines 347-413) executes before Step 6 `verify_peer_credentials` (line 422) and Step 6b `session_validator.is_active_session` (line 445), so the only check applied is `req.validate()`. It calls `pipe.camera.notify_activity()` (line 349) and returns `frame.data.clone()` (line 375) of the full raw frame. Socket is 0660 root:soos (config.rs:30-32). ARCHITECTURE.md:303 lists "Transmitting camera frames or biometric embeddings across the IPC socket" as a pitfall to avoid; AI/DECISIONS.md has no ADR authorising preview. GUI sends `deadline_monotonic_ns: u64::MAX` and `request_id: [0x5A; 32]` every 33 ms (ipc_camera.rs:118-125, 199).
- **Impact**: Any process running under any UID in the `soos` group (i.e. every enrolled user, including one who is not at the console) can silently stream the webcam at 30 fps from the root daemon, and keeps the camera awake (privacy LED on) indefinitely. Threat model (ARCHITECTURE.md:36) explicitly includes processes running under an attacker UID.
- **Recommendation**: Gate preview behind `[preview] enabled = false` in daemon.toml (default off, enabled by installer only if GUI installed), require `peer.uid == req.uid_hint` AND an active logind session for that uid (reuse SessionValidator), allow one preview client at a time, and record an ADR. Tests: `dispatcher_tests::test_preview_frame_rejected_without_active_session`, `test_preview_frame_rejected_when_disabled`, `test_preview_frame_uid_mismatch_rejected`.
- **Verifier notes**: Re-read crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:294-413: handle_request runs req.validate() (Step 5a), then Status (5b), then the PreviewFrame block (5c, lines 347-413) which calls pipe.camera.notify_activity(), clones frame.data and returns before Step 6 verify_peer_credentials (line ~422) and Step 6b session check (line ~445). crates/protocol/src/types.rs:312-325: validate() only checks version and service length. `grep -n preview crates/daemon/src/config.rs` -> no gating key; `grep -i preview AI/DECISIONS.md` -> no ADR. config.rs:28-32 socket 0660 group soos; packaging/soos-daemon.service RuntimeDirectoryMode=0750, so reachability is exactly 'any soos-group member'. AI/ARCHITECTURE.md:303 forbids frames over IPC and :36 lists attacker-UID processes in the threat model. Only nuance: read_and_process (dispatcher.rs:216-226) routes to handle_request when uid_hint == peer.uid or when the payload does not also decode as an Event, so a caller can trivially satisfy it; no mitigation exists. Existing tests dispatcher_tests.rs:540-640 only cover the no-pipeline (format 255) path.

#### CAM-09 — MiniFASNet live-class index differs between daemon (2), enrollment CLI (2) and GUI (default 1): GUI liveness display disagrees with daemon verdicts

GitHub issue: #146

**CRITICAL** · CONFIRMED · bug · effort S · (reviewer severity: MAJOR)

- **Locations**: `crates/daemon/src/pipeline.rs:189-193`, `crates/enrollment-cli/src/service.rs:773`, `crates/gui/src/main.rs:92`, `crates/inference-ort/src/pad.rs:82`, `AI/DECISIONS.md:19`
- **Evidence**: pipeline.rs:189-193 `OrtPadDetector::new_with_class_index(pad_session, config.vision.pad_threshold, 2)`; service.rs:773 `new_with_class_index(pad_session, 0.80, 2)`; gui/main.rs:92 `OrtPadDetector::new(pad_session, 0.80)` which uses `DEFAULT_MINIFASNET_LIVE_CLASS_INDEX = 1` (pad.rs:82). project-facts §4 says index 1 [72]; DECISIONS.md still says 0. (Cross-area with the anti-spoofing reviewer; reported here because it is in the GUI/daemon files in scope and directly explains GUI-vs-PAM disagreement.)
- **Impact**: The GUI's "live" indicator and guided enrollment liveness gate (worker.rs:78-84) use a different class than the daemon; users see "live" in the GUI and get "spoof detected" at the lock screen or vice-versa.
- **Recommendation**: Single constant consumed by all three binaries (remove hard-coded literals), validated against the real ONNX by the existing fixture test; update DECISIONS.md. Test: invariant test grepping that `new_with_class_index(` is only called with the shared constant.
- **Verifier notes**: Literals verified: crates/daemon/src/pipeline.rs:189-193 passes 2; crates/enrollment-cli/src/service.rs:773 passes 2; crates/gui/src/main.rs:92 uses OrtPadDetector::new -> DEFAULT_MINIFASNET_LIVE_CLASS_INDEX = 1 (pad.rs:82,93-98). The finding's framing is inverted: the project's own contract says 1 is correct (pad_tests.rs:298 test_pad_default_live_class_index_is_one; project-facts §4 line 76; walkthrough 72:20-37 states index 2 = ScreenReplay and that treating it as live enabled screen-replay attacks). History: 084ce5c (#127) set daemon/CLI/GUI to 2 with no rationale in the commit body; 72469ba (#135) changed the constant back to 1 with the empirical replay-attack rationale but did not touch pipeline.rs:192 / service.rs:773 (git log -L confirms 084ce5c is the last change there); 94d9ebe (#140, walkthrough 75:25,58) fixed only the GUI. So the authentication path (daemon) currently reads p_live from the class documented as ScreenReplay, and the enrollment CLI liveness gate does the same: a phone-screen replay would score as live at the lock screen. That is a PAD security-invariant break, not a GUI display disagreement, hence CRITICAL. Caveat: no real-ONNX test exists (pad_tests only exercise interpret_probabilities with synthetic vectors; `grep -rln minifasnet crates/*/tests` finds only manifest/registry tests), so the recommendation's 'validated against the real ONNX by the existing fixture test' is a wrong assumption; that test must be written (model present at /var/lib/soos/models/minifasnet_v2_80x80.onnx). DECISIONS.md:19 still says 0 (stale).

#### CAM-02 — GUI direct-V4L2 fallback silently fights the daemon for the device (EBUSY ping-pong) and EBUSY on S_FMT is not classified

GitHub issue: #150

**MAJOR** · CONFIRMED · bug · effort M

- **Locations**: `crates/gui/src/main.rs:59-82`, `crates/gui/src/ipc_camera.rs:58-61`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:292-300`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/error.rs:119-125`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:161-181`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:392-403`, `scripts/install.sh:366`
- **Evidence**: main.rs:61 chooses IPC only if `daemon_sock.exists() && IpcCameraManager::probe(daemon_sock)`; probe is `UnixStream::connect(..).is_ok()` (ipc_camera.rs:60). A user not yet in the `soos` group (install.sh only prints `soos-admin add-user` as a manual step, line 366; grep of Docs/ and scripts/ for re-login/newgrp returned nothing) gets EACCES and falls into direct mode while the daemon is running. `Device::with_path` succeeds on a UVC node held by another process (O_RDWR|O_NONBLOCK, v4l device.rs:64) and the conflict surfaces at `Capture::set_format` (v4l_impl.rs:293) which is mapped to `CameraError::SetFormat{reason}` — `from_io_error` (error.rs:119-125) only maps open() errors, so EBUSY is never `DeviceBusy`. The supervisor just warns and backs off 100 ms→5 s (v4l_impl.rs:165-181). Meanwhile the daemon releases the device after `idle_timeout` 10 s (v4l_impl.rs:392-403), the GUI grabs it, and the next PAM wake fails with EBUSY → `CameraUnavailable` → PAM_IGNORE.
- **Impact**: Exactly the reported symptom: GUI intermittently shows only the spinner, and face unlock randomly falls back to password while the GUI is open. Nothing is shown to the user and nothing is logged (see CAM-07).
- **Recommendation**: (1) Map EBUSY/ENODEV from set_format/REQBUFS/STREAMON through `from_io_error` (keep raw_os_error instead of `e.to_string()`); (2) probe must distinguish EACCES ("add user to soos group, re-login") from ENOENT/ECONNREFUSED (daemon stopped → direct mode allowed) and never go direct while `systemctl is-active soos-daemon` is true; (3) surface state in the GUI. Tests: `error_recovery_tests::test_set_format_ebusy_maps_to_device_busy`; gui test `test_probe_classifies_eacces_as_permission_denied`; gui state-machine test `test_direct_mode_refused_while_daemon_active`.
- **Verifier notes**: crates/gui/src/main.rs:59-82 and ipc_camera.rs:58-61 verified as quoted. Host check as a non-soos user: `ls /run/soos/` -> 'Permission denied' (dir 0750), so `daemon_sock.exists()` is already false before probe(): direct mode is chosen regardless of daemon state (same outcome as the EACCES path described). scripts/install.sh:366 only prints 'soos-admin add-user'; `grep -rni 're-login|newgrp|log out|relogin'` over scripts/, Docs/, packaging/, crates/admin-cli/src -> no match. Vendored v4l-0.14.0/src/device.rs:38,64 open with O_RDWR|O_NONBLOCK. crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:256-257 is the only from_io_error call; set_format (293-300), StreamCreate (305-311) and BufferDequeue (351-354) stringify the error, so EBUSY there never becomes DeviceBusy. Supervisor backoff v4l_impl.rs:161-181; idle release at 392-402 with default idle_timeout 10 s (config.rs:48). The EBUSY-on-S_FMT behaviour is standard uvcvideo privilege acquisition; I did not reproduce it live (daemon is inactive on this host).

#### CAM-03 — Daemon auto-selection binds to /dev/videoN, never re-enumerates, and retries a sentinel path forever when no camera exists at boot

GitHub issue: #151

**MAJOR** · CONFIRMED · reliability · effort M

- **Locations**: `crates/daemon/src/pipeline.rs:122-151`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/sensor.rs:137-160`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:125-187`, `packaging/soos-daemon.service:4-5`, `AI/ARCHITECTURE.md:165`, `AI/ARCHITECTURE.md:304`
- **Evidence**: `enumerate_capture_devices` builds `PathBuf::from(format!("/dev/{name}"))` (sensor.rs:138) — never a by-id link. pipeline.rs:124-140 resolves once at startup; if `select_camera_device` returns None the config keeps `/dev/v4l/by-id/default-camera` and `V4lCameraManager::spawn` still succeeds (thread spawn only). `run_v4l_supervisor` loops on the fixed `config.device_path` (v4l_impl.rs:135) with no re-resolution. The unit only has `After=local-fs.target systemd-udevd.service` (no device unit / udev settle). Also pipeline.rs:126 `|| !camera_cfg.device_path.exists()` silently substitutes a different camera when the configured by-id link has not appeared yet. ARCHITECTURE.md:165 states the daemon uses stable by-id paths and :304 forbids assuming `/dev/video0` is static.
- **Impact**: Boot race or USB re-enumeration after suspend/replug leaves the daemon locked on a non-existent node or a different camera (RGB instead of IR) until restart; health still reports camera ready (CAM-05). Direct cause of "camera sometimes detected wrongly".
- **Recommendation**: Make the supervisor take a `dyn CameraResolver` and re-resolve on every DeviceNotFound/UnsupportedCapability backoff; return by-id paths from enumeration when available; add `Wants=dev-video0.device`-style ordering or accept late arrival via re-resolution. Tests: `error_recovery_tests::test_supervisor_reresolves_device_after_enodev` (fake resolver returns None then Some), `pipeline_init_tests::test_pipeline_init_keeps_configured_by_id_when_missing_and_retries`.
- **Verifier notes**: crates/camera-v4l/src/sensor.rs:138 builds PathBuf::from(format!("/dev/{name}")) (never by-id). crates/daemon/src/pipeline.rs:122-140 resolves once; if select_camera_device returns None the sentinel path is kept and V4lCameraManager::spawn (v4l_impl.rs:31-67) only spawns a thread, so startup 'succeeds'. run_v4l_supervisor (v4l_impl.rs:125-187) loops on the fixed config.device_path with no re-resolution. pipeline.rs:126 `|| !camera_cfg.device_path.exists()` substitutes a different device when a configured by-id link is not yet present. packaging/soos-daemon.service:4-5 has only After=local-fs.target systemd-udevd.service. AI/ARCHITECTURE.md:165 ('by stable hardware ID /dev/v4l/by-id/...') and :304 verified.

#### CAM-04 — Three divergent resolvers: enrollment CLI / GUI-direct can pick a different camera (RGB) than the daemon (IR), so templates and auth use different sensors

GitHub issue: #152

**MAJOR** · CONFIRMED · bug · effort M

- **Locations**: `crates/enrollment-cli/src/service.rs:59-157`, `crates/daemon/src/pipeline.rs:124-140`, `crates/daemon/src/config.rs:239-243`, `crates/gui/src/main.rs:68-71`
- **Evidence**: service.rs: with no `camera_device` key (or no /etc/soos/daemon.toml at all — `grep -rn daemon.toml scripts/ packaging/ Docs/` finds no provisioning), `configured_device` is None so auto-detection at 116-123 is skipped and the function returns the first *sorted* `/dev/v4l/by-id/` entry (127-139), ignoring `sensor_preference`. Host listing shows by-id contains both `...-video-index0` and `...-video-index1` (metadata) links, and with two cameras the alphabetical winner is arbitrary. The daemon with the same missing key uses `DaemonConfig::default()` → sentinel → `select_camera_device(.., PreferIr)` (pipeline.rs:125-130). config.rs:240 treats "auto"/"" as sentinel but not "default" (works only by accident through `!exists()`). GUI direct mode calls the CLI resolver (main.rs:68-71).
- **Impact**: Enrolment captured on the RGB node, authentication on the IR node (or vice-versa) → systematic low cosine scores / PAD failures that look like a recognition problem; behaviour changes depending on whether daemon.toml exists.
- **Recommendation**: One shared resolver in soos-camera-v4l used by all three binaries (see improvement plan step 1) with identical sentinel handling and identical fallback order; installer provisions `/etc/soos/daemon.toml` with `camera_device = "auto"` and `sensor_preference`. Tests (hermetic, fake enumerator): `resolver_tests::test_no_config_prefers_ir_for_daemon_cli_and_gui`, `test_by_id_metadata_index1_never_selected`, `test_default_sentinel_handled_like_auto`.
- **Verifier notes**: crates/enrollment-cli/src/service.rs:59-157 re-read: with no config file or no camera_device key, configured_device is None, the auto-detect block (116-123) is skipped, and the first sorted /dev/v4l/by-id entry (127-139) wins, ignoring sensor_preference. The CLI does pass /etc/soos/daemon.toml (service.rs:731-733), so it converges with the daemon only when that file exists with the key. `grep -rn daemon.toml scripts packaging Docs` -> no provisioning anywhere (packaging/debian/postinst, packaging/arch/soos.install do not write it either). crates/daemon/src/config.rs:239-243 treats only "auto"/"" as sentinel; "default" works only via !exists() at pipeline.rs:126. GUI direct mode calls the CLI resolver (main.rs:68-71). Host: /dev/v4l/by-id has ...-video-index0 -> video0 and ...-video-index1 -> video1 (video1 is META_CAPTURE only per v4l2-ctl), confirming by-id listing includes non-capture nodes.

#### CAM-05 — HealthState.camera_ready is set to true unconditionally at startup; `soos-admin status` lies when the camera is dead

GitHub issue: #153

**MAJOR** · CONFIRMED · reliability · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/daemon/src/main.rs:59-64`, `crates/daemon/src/health.rs:53-54`, `crates/admin-cli/src/status.rs:85-88`
- **Evidence**: main.rs:61-63: `Ok(comps) => { health.set_camera_ready(true); ... }` immediately after `initialize_pipeline`, which only spawns the capture thread (V4lCameraManager::spawn returns Ok before any open). `grep -n set_camera_ready crates/daemon/src/*.rs` shows no other writer; `CameraManager::is_ready()` is never polled into health.
- **Impact**: Diagnostics report "Camera Capture: ready" during permanent DeviceNotFound/EBUSY backoff, hiding CAM-02/CAM-03 from the user and from support scripts.
- **Recommendation**: Spawn a 500 ms health poller: `health.set_camera_ready(camera.is_ready())` plus a `camera_status()` string (device, format, state, last error). Test: `health_tests::test_camera_ready_false_when_device_missing` using `V4lCameraManager::spawn` on a nonexistent path.
- **Verifier notes**: crates/daemon/src/main.rs:60-64: `Ok(comps) => { health.set_camera_ready(true); health.set_models_verified(true); comps }` immediately after initialize_pipeline. `grep -rn set_camera_ready crates/daemon/src` -> only main.rs:63 writes it. health.rs:53-54,65-67: camera_ready feeds is_healthy. V4lCameraManager::spawn returns Ok before any device open (v4l_impl.rs:31-67), so a daemon in permanent DeviceNotFound backoff reports camera ready and healthy via StatusResponse (dispatcher.rs:332) and soos-admin status (status.rs:85-88).

#### CAM-06 — GUI forks `systemctl is-active` on every repaint and runs pkexec synchronously on the UI thread; camera source is chosen once and never switches after Pause/Resume

GitHub issue: #154

**MAJOR** · CONFIRMED · bug · effort M

- **Locations**: `crates/gui/src/app.rs:72-105`, `crates/gui/src/app.rs:226-236`, `crates/gui/src/app.rs:1080-1110`, `crates/gui/src/worker.rs:157`, `crates/gui/src/main.rs:59-82`, `crates/gui/src/ipc_camera.rs:103-111`
- **Evidence**: `render_header` (called from `ui()` each frame, app.rs:1110) executes `Self::is_daemon_active()` = `Command::new("systemctl").args(["is-active",...]).status()` (app.rs:73-79, 226). The worker calls `egui_ctx.request_repaint()` per camera frame (worker.rs:157) → ~30 fork/exec per second on the UI thread. `pause_daemon`/`resume_daemon` block the UI thread on `pkexec ... .status()` (app.rs:82-105) from the click handler (229-236). The camera manager is selected once in main.rs:60-82; after Pause, `IpcCameraManager` loops on `UnixStream::connect` failure every 200 ms (ipc_camera.rs:104-111) forever; after Resume in direct mode the daemon collides with the GUI (CAM-02).
- **Impact**: UI stutter/freezes ("GUI sometimes fails"), Polkit dialog freezes the window, and the video feed goes permanently blank after using Pause; Resume while in direct mode breaks PAM until the GUI exits.
- **Recommendation**: Move daemon-state polling to a background thread (2 s interval, atomic flag), run pkexec on a thread with a result channel, and implement a `CameraSource` state machine that re-probes and swaps between IPC and direct managers (worker takes `Arc<ArcSwap<Arc<dyn CameraManager>>>`). Tests: inject a `DaemonController` trait and assert `ui()` never calls it synchronously; `test_camera_source_switches_to_ipc_when_daemon_returns`.
- **Verifier notes**: crates/gui/src/app.rs:204 `fn render_header`, line 226 `let daemon_active = Self::is_daemon_active();` (Command::new("systemctl").status(), lines 73-79) executed on every ui() pass (ui() calls render_header at app.rs:1110). worker.rs:157/186 request_repaint on every new frame, so the fork rate tracks the camera frame rate (~30/s in IPC mode). pause_daemon/resume_daemon (82-105) call pkexec .status() synchronously from the click handlers at 229-236. main.rs:60-82 selects the manager once; ipc_camera.rs:103-111 retries connect every 200 ms forever after the daemon stops, and there is no code path that swaps to V4lCameraManager. All as described.

#### CAM-07 — GUI never surfaces camera errors: no tracing subscriber is installed and all camera failures collapse into a generic spinner

GitHub issue: #155

**MAJOR** · CONFIRMED · reliability · effort M

- **Locations**: `crates/gui/src/main.rs:22-129`, `crates/gui/Cargo.toml:19-20`, `crates/gui/src/app.rs:1119-1125`, `crates/gui/src/ipc_camera.rs:107-111`, `crates/gui/src/ipc_camera.rs:131-165`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:165-170`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/manager.rs:7-22`
- **Evidence**: `grep -rn 'tracing_subscriber\|fmt::init' crates/gui/src/` → no match although `tracing-subscriber` is a declared dependency (Cargo.toml:20); every `tracing::warn!/info!` in the GUI process (including the V4L supervisor's `warn!("Camera error on ...")`, v4l_impl.rs:165) is dropped. `IpcCameraManager` swallows connect/read/decode errors with `is_ready=false; break` (ipc_camera.rs:107-111, 131-165, 192-195). The UI shows only `"Connecting to camera and initializing models..."` (app.rs:1123) whenever `latest_frame_slot` is empty. The `CameraManager` trait exposes no error/status accessor.
- **Impact**: Users cannot tell EBUSY from EACCES from a missing device from a paused daemon; support gets no logs.
- **Recommendation**: Init `tracing_subscriber::fmt()` (stderr, RUST_LOG) in main.rs; add `fn status(&self) -> CameraStatus` (default impl) to the trait with `Error{kind, since, retries}`, `Suspended`, `Warmup`, `Ready`; render device path, negotiated format and last error in the header. Tests: `layout_tests::test_status_banner_shows_camera_error` with `MockCameraManager::set_error(Simulated{EBUSY})`.
- **Verifier notes**: `grep -rn 'tracing_subscriber|fmt::init|with_env_filter' crates/gui/src/` -> exit 1 (no match) while crates/gui/Cargo.toml:20 declares tracing-subscriber; main.rs:22-129 never installs a subscriber, so tracing::info!/warn! in the GUI process (including the V4L supervisor warn! at v4l_impl.rs:165-170 when running direct) is dropped. ipc_camera.rs:107-111,131-165,192-195 set is_ready=false and break without recording any reason. app.rs:1119-1125 shows only the spinner + 'Connecting to camera and initializing models...'. manager.rs:7-22 CameraManager trait has no status/error accessor. Borderline MINOR by rubric, kept MAJOR because it turns every camera failure into an undiagnosable permanent spinner, which is the user-reported symptom.

#### CAM-08 — GUI writes the plaintext biometric embedding to a predictable temp file during the pkexec import and silently falls back to /tmp key/store

GitHub issue: #156

**MAJOR** · CONFIRMED · security · effort M

- **Locations**: `crates/gui/src/app.rs:842-870`, `crates/gui/src/main.rs:26-42`
- **Evidence**: app.rs:842-850: `tmp_path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(".soos_gui_import_{}_{}.json", uid, pid))`; `serde_json::to_string(&fused_embedding)` then `std::fs::write(&tmp_path, json)` (default umask, no O_EXCL/0600); the file lives until `pkexec soos-enroll import` returns (user interaction window), then `remove_file` (860-864). main.rs:30-31/38-40: on any store/key error the GUI silently uses `temp_dir()/soos-gui-master.key` and `soos-gui-biometrics`. DECISIONS.md [2026-09-16] requires embeddings to be zeroized/protected; ARCHITECTURE.md:303 forbids embeddings in clear outside the store. (Cross-area with the security reviewer; confirmed from the files in scope.)
- **Impact**: Another local user can read the 512-D template from /tmp while the Polkit dialog is open (or pre-create the path); users end up with templates in /tmp believing they enrolled into the system store.
- **Recommendation**: Pipe the embedding to `pkexec soos-enroll import --stdin` via stdin (or memfd) and zeroize; replace the silent /tmp fallback with an explicit `--dev-store <dir>` flag and a visible banner. Tests: `test_import_reads_embedding_from_stdin`, gui test asserting no file is created under temp_dir during finalize.
- **Verifier notes**: crates/gui/src/app.rs:842-864 verified: temp_dir().join(".soos_gui_import_{uid}_{pid}.json"), serde_json::to_string(&fused_embedding), std::fs::write (default umask, no O_EXCL/0600, no zeroize of the JSON string despite `use zeroize::Zeroizing` at app.rs:26), file removed only after pkexec returns. main.rs:26-42: MasterKey/BiometricStore failures silently fall back to temp_dir()/soos-gui-master.key and soos-gui-biometrics. crates/enrollment-cli/src/args.rs:144-146: import only accepts `--file PathBuf`, no stdin option exists. Cross-area duplicate with the security reviewer is acknowledged; kept MAJOR (embedding exposure to co-local users, not an auth bypass).

#### CAM-10 — Capture path trusts requested format: driver-returned fourcc, bytesperline and V4L2 error flags are ignored; frames are not size-validated

GitHub issue: #192

**MINOR** · CONFIRMED · reliability · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:272-303`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:332-388`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/frame.rs:63-81`, `crates/vision/src/color.rs:39-55`
- **Evidence**: Only `actual_format.width/height` are read (v4l_impl.rs:302-303); `actual_format.fourcc` and `bytesperline` are unused and the frame is labelled `target_format` (line 381). When `enum_formats` fails, `config.format` (YUYV) is requested blindly (279-281) though the driver may substitute. `meta.flags` (V4L2_BUF_FLAG_ERROR) is never checked (332-373). `Frame::new` performs no size check; vision rejects mismatched buffers per frame (color.rs:41-55), so corrupted/padded frames become silent drops.
- **Impact**: On drivers that pad lines or substitute formats the GUI shows nothing and the daemon returns NoFace/Unavailable with no diagnostic; a single ERROR-flagged UVC frame can feed garbage to detection.
- **Recommendation**: Validate `actual_format.fourcc == fourcc` (else `SetFormat` error), reject `bytesperline != width*bpp` (or de-stride), skip frames with ERROR flag, and check `expected_buffer_size` for uncompressed formats before publishing. Tests: pure-function unit tests `test_validate_captured_buffer_rejects_short_yuyv`, `test_error_flag_frame_skipped` (mock).
- **Verifier notes**: v4l_impl.rs:302-303 read only actual_format.width/height; fourcc and bytesperline are unused; frame labelled target_format at 381. 273-288: enum_formats failure -> config.format requested blindly. 332-373: meta.flags never inspected; only bytesused slicing. frame.rs:63-81 Frame::new has no size check; vision/src/color.rs:39-55 rejects wrong buffer sizes per frame (silent drop at the consumer).

#### CAM-11 — `fps` / `idle_fps` are never applied to the hardware (no VIDIOC_S_PARM); only the mock honours them

GitHub issue: #193

**MINOR** · CONFIRMED · dead-code · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/camera-v4l/src/config.rs:23-26`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:248-407`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/mock.rs:71`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/mock.rs:143`, `Docs/CAMERA_V4L_CRATE.md:51-52`
- **Evidence**: `grep -n 'fps\|set_params' crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs` → no matches; mock.rs:71/143 use `cfg.fps`/`cfg.idle_fps`. Docs list them as effective settings.
- **Impact**: Mock/production divergence; the real camera runs at the driver default (e.g. 10 fps for large YUYV modes), which raises the odds of `MAX_FRAME_AGE_NS` (150 ms) staleness and lowers preview FPS.
- **Recommendation**: Call `Capture::set_params` with `Parameters::with_fps(config.fps)` after set_format and log the granted rate; drop `idle_fps` or implement it. Test: mock parity test + a `#[ignore]` hardware test.
- **Verifier notes**: `grep -n 'fps|set_params' crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs` -> no matches; mock.rs:71 and :143 consume cfg.fps/cfg.idle_fps. Docs/CAMERA_V4L_CRATE.md:50-51 list fps/idle_fps as effective settings. MAX_FRAME_AGE_NS = 150_000_000 at crates/daemon/src/pipeline.rs:16, used at dispatcher.rs:662, so a driver-default low frame rate does increase staleness risk.

#### CAM-12 — DQBUF poll timeout is 2000 ms while the comment and Criterion C9 promise ≤250 ms / Drop < 500 ms; shutdown can stall 2 s

GitHub issue: #194

**MINOR** · CONFIRMED · reliability · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:313-317`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:108-116`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/shutdown_tests.rs:24-48`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/shutdown_tests.rs:92-93`
- **Evidence**: `let stream_timeout_ms = 2000;` directly under a comment stating "clamped to 150-250ms, ensuring Drop completes in < 500ms (Criterion C9)". `Drop` joins the worker (108-116) which may be inside `poll()` for up to 2 s (v4l mmap stream.rs:163). shutdown_tests only measure the mock (24-48) and spawn-without-device (92).
- **Impact**: GUI close and daemon stop/restart can hang up to 2 s on a stalled camera; C9 is not actually verified for the production manager.
- **Recommendation**: Use a 250 ms poll timeout and treat consecutive timeouts (e.g. 8) as `Starved`; keep `running` checks between polls. Test: hardware `#[ignore]` drop-latency test plus a mock parity assertion on the constant.
- **Verifier notes**: v4l_impl.rs:313-317: comment promises 150-250 ms / C9 < 500 ms, code sets stream_timeout_ms = 2000. Vendored v4l-0.14.0/src/io/mmap/stream.rs:163 polls with that timeout; `running` is only re-checked after stream.next() returns (v4l_impl.rs:331-343). Drop (108-116) joins the worker. shutdown_tests.rs:24-48 measure the mock; :86-100 measure a nonexistent-device backoff, so the production streaming Drop latency is unverified. Practical impact limited to a stalled camera (a healthy 30 fps stream returns within ~33 ms).

#### CAM-13 — IR/RGB classification relies on truncated-name and format heuristics; identical card names and unmapped IR fourccs defeat it

GitHub issue: #195

**MINOR** · CONFIRMED · design · effort M

- **Locations**: `crates/camera-v4l/src/sensor.rs:48-83`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_impl.rs:226-235`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/sensor.rs:29-38`
- **Evidence**: `lower.ends_with(" i")` (sensor.rs:60) is the only defence against 31-char truncation; classification then falls to "any colour fourcc ⇒ Rgb" (66-74). Host probe shows both nodes report the identical card name `Integrated Camera: Integrated C`. `fourcc_to_pixel_format` maps only GREY/Y800/Y8 (v4l_impl.rs:232); IR sensors exposing Y8I, Y16, Y10, Y12 yield empty `supported_formats` and are dropped by `enumerate_capture_devices` (sensor.rs:154). `CameraDeviceInfo` carries no `bus_info`, `driver`, by-id name or frame-size signature.
- **Impact**: IR cameras that advertise YUYV (common on Windows-Hello modules) are classified RGB; Y16/Y8I IR cameras are invisible; PreferIr then silently picks the RGB node.
- **Recommendation**: Extend `CameraDeviceInfo` with `bus_info`, by-id link name, `device_caps`, frame sizes; classify with an ordered scorer (by-id name contains "IR"/"Infrared" > card name > grey-only formats > small square/360p frame sizes), map Y8I/Y16/Y10/Y12 to Grey-family formats. Test: table-driven `classify_tests` with real-world names (Asus G14, ThinkPad, Dell, Surface), and `test_enumerate_keeps_y16_only_ir_node` via a fake enumerator.
- **Verifier notes**: sensor.rs:48-83 heuristics verified (ends_with(" i") at :60; any colour fourcc -> Rgb at :66-74). v4l_impl.rs:226-235 maps only GREY/Y800/'Y8  ' to Grey; Y16/Y8I/Y10/Y12 -> None -> empty supported_formats -> dropped at sensor.rs:154. Host: /sys/class/video4linux/video0 and video1 both report 'Integrated Camera: Integrated C' (identical, truncated). Vendored v4l capability.rs:101 maps device_caps into `capabilities`, so the META_CAPTURE-only node is correctly excluded by the VIDEO_CAPTURE check; the finding does not claim otherwise. CameraDeviceInfo (sensor.rs:29-38) has only path/card_name/supported_formats.

#### CAM-14 — Preview frames above 2 MiB make the daemon drop the connection each time, putting the GUI into a 100 ms reconnect loop

GitHub issue: #196

**MINOR** · CONFIRMED · reliability · effort S

- **Locations**: `crates/daemon/src/dispatcher.rs:361-396`, `crates/protocol/src/types.rs:21`, `crates/protocol/src/codec.rs:88-96`, `crates/gui/src/ipc_camera.rs:192-203`
- **Evidence**: `encode_preview(&preview_resp)?` (dispatcher.rs:391) propagates `CodecError::MessageTooLarge` (codec.rs:91-96) as a DaemonError, which closes the connection (handle_connection line 151-153). A 1920×1080 YUYV frame is 4,147,200 B > `MAX_PREVIEW_MESSAGE_SIZE` 2 MiB; the frame size is whatever the driver granted (v4l_impl.rs:302). The GUI treats the EOF as a transient error and reconnects every 100 ms (ipc_camera.rs:202-203) with no message. Every request also clones the full frame (dispatcher.rs:375) at 30 Hz.
- **Impact**: On cameras that do not grant 640×480, the daemon-proxied GUI preview never works and the daemon log fills with errors 10×/s.
- **Recommendation**: Downscale/convert daemon-side to ≤640-px-wide RGB (or JPEG) before encoding, and on oversize return an explicit `PreviewResponse{format:255}` instead of an error. Tests: `dispatcher_tests::test_preview_oversize_frame_returns_empty_response_not_disconnect`.
- **Verifier notes**: dispatcher.rs:391 `encode_preview(&preview_resp)?`; codec.rs:78-80 -> encode_with_limit(MAX_PREVIEW_MESSAGE_SIZE = 2 MiB, types.rs:21) returns CodecError::MessageTooLarge (codec.rs:90-96); daemon error.rs:31 `Codec(#[from] CodecError)`; handle_connection dispatcher.rs:151-153 `Ok(Err(e)) => return Err(e)` closes the connection. ipc_camera.rs:137-139 read failure -> break -> 100 ms sleep (202-203) -> reconnect. Frame size is whatever S_FMT granted (v4l_impl.rs:302); 640x480 YUYV (614,400 B) fits, 1280x720 YUYV (1,843,200 B) fits, 1920x1080 YUYV (4,147,200 B) does not. Full-frame clone per request at dispatcher.rs:375 verified.

#### CAM-15 — Camera docs and verification matrix cite wrong defaults and non-existent tests

GitHub issue: #197

**MINOR** · PARTIAL · doc-drift · effort S

- **Locations**: `Docs/CAMERA_V4L_CRATE.md:48-54`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/config.rs:44-49`, `Docs/ENROLLMENT_CLI.md:79`, `Docs/ENROLLMENT_CLI.md:90`, `AI/VERIFICATION_MATRIX.md:382`, `AI/VERIFICATION_MATRIX.md:395`, `AI/VERIFICATION_MATRIX.md:427`, `AI/BACKLOG.md:1819`, `crates/daemon/src/config.rs:244`, `AI/walkthroughs/73_gdm_lockscreen_feedback_multi_frame.md:36`
- **Evidence**: Docs say `auto_format` default false (code: true, config.rs:44), `idle_timeout` 60 s (code: 10 s, config.rs:48), C5 "drops 15–30 frames" while any daemon.toml sets `warmup_frames.unwrap_or(0)` (config.rs:244). ENROLLMENT_CLI.md:79/90 claim by-id resolution although auto-detection returns /dev/videoN. `grep -rln 'fn test_pipeline_init_device_selection_prefers_ir\|fn test_v4l2_31_char_truncated_name_classified_as_ir\|fn test_resolve_camera_device_auto_resolution\b\|fn test_resolve_camera_device_default_resolution\|fn test_enumerate_filters_empty_format_nodes' crates tests` → no files, yet matrix rows CAM1, ASG3, GARP1 mark them ✅ Verified. Walkthrough 73 says "default" is a sentinel in daemon config; config.rs:240 only handles "auto"/"".
- **Impact**: Reviewers and agents trust acceptance evidence that does not exist; operators tune settings that do not behave as documented.
- **Recommendation**: Fix the defaults table, rename/add the missing tests, add ADR entries for /dev/videoN auto-selection and preview streaming. Test: extend tests/invariants with a check that every backticked `test_*` name in AI/VERIFICATION_MATRIX.md exists in the tree.
- **Verifier notes**: Doc drift confirmed: Docs/CAMERA_V4L_CRATE.md:48 auto_format 'default: false' vs config.rs:44 true; :52 idle_timeout '60s' vs config.rs:48 10 s; C5 row vs daemon config.rs:244 warmup_frames.unwrap_or(0); Docs/ENROLLMENT_CLI.md:79,90 by-id claims vs /dev/videoN auto-detection; walkthrough 73:36 'default' sentinel vs config.rs:240 (auto/'' only). The 'non-existent tests' part is overstated: `grep -rn 'truncated|prefers_ir|auto_resolution|default_resolution|filters_empty' crates/*/tests` shows 3 of the 5 matrix names are naming drift of tests that DO exist: dual_sensor_tests.rs:164 test_sensor_classification_truncated_ir_card_name (matrix: test_v4l2_31_char_truncated_name_classified_as_ir), device_resolution_tests.rs:21 test_resolve_camera_device_auto_resolution_with_config (matrix: ..._auto_resolution), daemon config_tests.rs:218 test_pipeline_config_camera_device_auto_resolution (closest to CAM1's test_pipeline_init_device_selection_prefers_ir, though it tests config parsing, not pipeline init). Only test_resolve_camera_device_default_resolution and test_enumerate_filters_empty_format_nodes are genuinely missing. Recommendation should say 'rename 3, add 2'.

#### CAM-16 — No hermetic test covers enumeration, selection, open/stream or the GUI failure paths; existing resolution tests depend on host hardware

GitHub issue: #198

**MINOR** · CONFIRMED · test-gap · effort M

- **Locations**: `crates/enrollment-cli/tests/device_resolution_tests.rs:21-47`, `crates/daemon/tests/pipeline_init_tests.rs:48`, `crates/daemon/tests/pipeline_init_tests.rs:192-194`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/hotunplug_tests.rs:15-60`, `crates/gui/tests/layout_tests.rs:214-268`, `crates/camera-v4l/src/sensor.rs:118`
- **Evidence**: device_resolution_tests call the real `enumerate_capture_devices()` and only assert `resolved.starts_with("/dev")`, so they pass whichever camera (or `/dev/video0` fallback) is chosen. pipeline_init_tests use `use_mock_camera: true` only. hotunplug/shutdown/warmup tests use `MockCameraManager`. `grep -rn 'enumerate_capture_devices\|V4lCameraManager::spawn' crates/*/tests tests/` → only shutdown_tests.rs:92. The GUI has one IPC happy-path test and none for EACCES/EBUSY/daemon-paused/oversize.
- **Impact**: Wrong-camera regressions (CAM-03/04/13) and GUI failure modes (CAM-02/06/07) cannot be caught in CI.
- **Recommendation**: Introduce `trait DeviceEnumerator` with a fake implementation and rewrite resolution tests against fixture tables; add GUI state-machine tests with a fake probe; add `#[ignore]`d hardware smoke tests runnable via `SOOS_HW_TESTS=1`.
- **Verifier notes**: device_resolution_tests.rs:21-47 asserts only `resolved != "auto"` and starts_with("/dev") against the real enumerator. `grep -rn 'enumerate_capture_devices|V4lCameraManager::spawn' crates/*/tests tests/` -> only shutdown_tests.rs:92 (nonexistent device). pipeline_init_tests use use_mock_camera; sensor.rs:118 enumerate_capture_devices has no injectable enumerator. GUI has no tests for EACCES/EBUSY/paused/oversize paths (layout_tests only cover rendering and the IPC happy path).
